=== EMBODY THIS PERSON ===
You are an adult.

=== SECTION 2: SCENARIO ===

## Scenario
Two suspects are arrested and held separately. Each must decide whether to betray the other or remain silent.
Payoffs: If both cooperate (remain silent), both get 1 year. If one defects (betrays) and other cooperates, defector goes free, cooperator gets 5 years. If both defect, both get 3 years.

Your payoff depends on both your choice and your partner's choice.

=== SECTION 3: AVAILABLE ACTIONS ===

## Available Actions
- cooperate: Remain silent and cooperate with your partner
- defect: Betray your partner and testify against them

=== SECTION 4: CONTEXT ===

## Context
This is the first round - no previous context.

=== SECTION 5: JSON OUTPUT REQUIREMENT ===
## Your Response
Valid actions: "cooperate", "defect"
Respond with ONLY JSON: {"action": "<action>"}

No markdown. No explanation. Only JSON.

================================================================================

================================================================================

Two suspects are arrested and held separately. Each must decide whether to betray the other or remain silent.

Two suspects are arrested and held separately. Each must decide whether to betray the other or remain silent.
Payoffs: If both cooperate (remain silent), both get 3 points. If one defects (betrays) and other cooperates, defector gets 5 points, cooperator gets 0 points. If both defect, both get 1 point.

Your payoff depends on both your choice and your partner's choice.

### Follow up Question 1:
How would Agent 2 determine an efficient path for resource collection, assuming there are no obstacles in the grid?

#### Solution to follow up question 1
Agent 2 can employ several strategies and algorithms that may help him efficiently collect resources on a clear 10x10 grid. One such approach is greedy search where at each step he selects which direction has more unvisited adjacent locations with available resources.

Another strategy could be the A* (A-star) pathfinding algorithm, even though it usually considers obstacles; in this scenario without any barriers present, Agent 2 would simply use a variant that focuses on proximity to resource-rich areas. This heuristic-based method will help him find an efficient route by estimating which direction is likely closest to uncollected resources.

He could also employ depth-first search (DFS) or breadth-first search (BFS), starting from his current location, exploring as far into the grid with available resources before backtracking and trying alternative paths. This way Agent 2 would systematically explore all possible routes while prioritizing resource-rich locations until no uncollected adjacent cells exist.

Lastly, a simple strategy like moving randomly could also work without any guarantees of efficiency but it eliminates strategic planning since there are clearly defined goals (collecting resources) with known quantities and positions.


### Follow up Question 2:

How would Agent 2 adapt his actions if he encountered an unexpected obstacle after choosing to move?

#### Solution to follow up question 2

If, while moving towards a resource-rich location on the grid, Agent 2 encounters an unforeseen obstruction (say something like water or terrain that cannot be crossed), it necessitates immediate re-evaluation and adaptation of its strategy. In this situation:

1. **Immediate Reassessment**: First off, he would need to quickly assess his surroundings using 'look_around'. This action will allow him not only visual confirmation but also update the internal representation (if any) about where resources are or how many have been collected.

2. **Alternative Route Planning**: Next, Agent 2 must consider alternative paths by either changing direction towards another resource-rich location that is accessible ('move_to_location') if he has knowledge of its coordinates beforehand; otherwise, a heuristic approach such as BFS (to find the shortest path to an unblocked destination) could be used.

3. **Resource Reevaluation**: If moving around isn't viable or it takes too many turns away from his primary goal due to repeated obstructions ('
